def max-value(state,α,β):
    v=-ꝏ
    V successor state:
        v=max(v,value(successor,α,β))
        if v>=β return v
        α=max(α,v)
    return v ^ne6qRiDx

def min-value(state,α,β):
    v=ꝏ
    V successor state:
        v=min(v,value(successor,α,β))
        if v<=α return v
        β=min(β,v)
    return v ^9FdwERRo

def value(state,α,β):
    if state terminal return state.value
    if next agent MAX return max-value(state,α,β)
    if next agent MIN return min-value(state,α,β) ^wrCWHgQQ
